Problems & improvements

问题与改进

每条按等级与类型标注，展开可看触发条件、实际观察、验收标准、证据原文与可直接粘贴的上游 Issue 草稿。评审发现不等于本轮实际阻塞，也不因业务通过而标为已解决。

独立评测发现

来源：独立评审 · 口径 v2 · 源 Run 38106778635 / attempt 1

最新上游状态：未复核。原评审“已解决”不作为上游修复证明；应用绕行、业务 QA 通过均不关闭框架问题。

等级与类型怎么定
  • 严重阻塞交付，或造成数据丢失、安全问题，且没有合理绕行
  • 重要按指引或公开 API 正常使用会静默得到错误结果，或只能用非公开手段绕行
  • 轻微能完成，但要多花排查成本或在应用侧绕行
  • 信息措辞、示例完整度等不影响结果的改进
  • 实现缺陷实现违背文档或公开约定
  • 能力缺口需求属于框架职责，但缺少能力或公开接入
  • 指引错误指引、示例或注释写错，或彼此矛盾，照做会出错
  • 指引缺失指引缺少必要说明或示例，需要读源码或试探
  • 易用性改进现有能力可用，但有改进空间
业务实现与执行环境观察 · 不计入框架得分（3）
重要未分类问题候选反馈：未复核原评审置信度：confirmed
F4 · 附件内容路由未按指引加认证即公开可读

Agent / 业务实现 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file · 原评审：未解决

⌄

未提供反馈复核记录，保留为候选反馈；原评审置信度不代表已排除误报。

file Skill 明确 content 路由公开，受限文件需应用对 <accessPath>/* 自行加认证授权；应用只对 /api 上传路由加了 auth.required()，流式内容路由仍为公开，任何持 URL 者可读取他人截图。

实际影响
未授权用户可通过 URL 读取他人截图，存在隐私泄露风险。
建议改法
按指引对 <accessPath>/* 内容路由加会话认证，或改为经应用自有鉴权路由代理读取。

本条未提供结构化触发条件、绕行与回归标准，请按原文和证据复核，不自动补造。

E26 E24
轻微易用性改进候选反馈：未复核原评审置信度：confirmed
F5 · 通知投递失败被静默吞掉

Agent / 业务实现 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-notification/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-notification · 原评审：未解决

⌄

未提供反馈复核记录，保留为候选反馈；原评审置信度不代表已排除误报。

notify() 以 .catch(()=>undefined) 结束，投递失败既无日志也无信号；与 Skill 强调的“保留投递诊断、区分已受理与已送达”不一致。

实际影响
超时提醒等异步投递失败不可见，运维难以发现通知中断。
建议改法
记录投递失败日志并暴露计数/指标，或在失败时提供受控重试。

本条未提供结构化触发条件、绕行与回归标准，请按原文和证据复核，不自动补造。

E1 E7
轻微未分类问题候选反馈：未复核原评审：待确认（suspected）
F6 · 超时提醒正向场景无法在验收环境构造

环境 / 外部服务 · 原评审：未解决

⌄

未提供反馈复核记录，保留为候选反馈；原评审置信度不代表已排除误报。

“处理中超过 24 小时”样本不存在，创建接口用 z.strictObject 拒绝 createdAt 回填，且无框架提供的时钟注入，导致超时提醒正向链路只能在单测验证、浏览器验收仅覆盖空态。

实际影响
时间型业务逻辑缺少端到端正向验证，潜在缺陷可能漏检。
建议改法
在应用侧提供可注入时钟或受控的回填命令；测试流程可约定时间注入方式。

本条未提供结构化触发条件、绕行与回归标准，请按原文和证据复核，不自动补造。

E69 E33
实现者过程与运行背景 · 4 个过程问题 / 4 条建议 · 不自动归为框架问题

本任务已处理只描述应用侧处理结果，不代表 NocoBase3 上游已修复。

本轮过程记录

Repaired the application-owned tests that the IT service desk changes left stale. (1) The template home-page test was rewritten to follow the new dashboard. (2) The header gained an inbox entry that reads data through the API client, so tests/components/layout-i18n.test.tsx — which renders AppLayout with a minimal fake app — was stubbed for the unread-count hook. Application source was correct in both cases; only the tests needed to follow the feature.

问题 01 · verification

tests/components/home.test.tsx imported a deleted capabilities module

本任务已处理

Vitest failed to resolve ../../client/pages/home/capabilities.js from tests/components/home.test.tsx. The whole home suite failed at import time, so the test run ended 1 failed suite / 228 passed (test exit 1). format, lint, typecheck, lockfile and build checks all passed.

原因（实现者自述）
The application intentionally replaced the template landing page with the IT service desk dashboard at '/', deleting client/pages/home/capabilities.ts, prompt-dialog.tsx and use-copy.ts. The template-owned tests/components/home.test.tsx still imported and exercised the removed capabilities UI, so it was obsolete rather than a product defect.
处理方式
Rewrote tests/components/home.test.tsx to test the dashboard home page: it mocks useApiClient and asserts the metric cards, pending/overdue ticket lists and engineer workload from itDashboard + itTickets, then covers the load-error/retry path and the 401 session-expired alert. No application source was changed.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 02 · feature-gap

In-app ticket notifications were sent but unreachable from the shell

本任务已处理

Header showed only Appearance and the account menu: no inbox bell, no /notifications page, and /main/notifications redirected to /main. The inbox API (/api/notificationInApp/messages) answered 200 with the correct target /tickets/1, so the data was produced; only a UI entry was missing.

原因（实现者自述）
The notification-in-app plugin was registered and the ticket service already sent inbox messages on assign/resolve/return/close, but nothing in the application shell rendered the inbox entry. The template header deliberately ships no Inbox entry, and no app-owned /inbox route existed to link to.
处理方式
Added the /inbox route (lazy pages/inbox.tsx, the plugin InboxPage wrapper) in client/routes.ts, and an InboxEntry in client/layouts/components/header-actions.tsx using the plugin useInboxUnreadCount() plus the shared @nocobase/inbox-button. Updated the template assertion in tests/logic/client-shell.test.tsx from 'no Inbox' to 'Inbox link present, no Settings', and added header-button/unread-badge tests to tests/components/inbox.test.tsx.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 03 · validation

Attachment upload stored non-image content and gave no feedback

本任务已处理

A file named corrupt.png declared image/png was accepted with HTTP 201 and stored, and an unsupported .exe was silently dropped by the picker accept filter with no explicit message.

原因（实现者自述）
The file plugin's upload path trusts the caller's File.type and has no content check; the app had no server-side validation of the bytes. The picker accept only filters the file dialog, so it neither guarantees what is uploaded nor explains the exclusion.
处理方式
Added validateAttachmentUpload middleware in server/routes/it-ticket-files.ts, mounted on uploadOne/uploadMany after auth.required(). It reads the multipart body from a clone (so the plugin size limit/parser still see it), verifies a real raster image by magic number (PNG/JPEG/GIF/WebP/BMP), and refuses non-image types with 400 TICKET_UNSUPPORTED_ATTACHMENT. Clarified tickets.form.attachmentsHint in both locales. Added an integration test covering accept and both rejection paths.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 04 · verification

tests/components/layout-i18n.test.tsx crashed after the header gained the inbox entry

本任务已处理

Vitest rendered AppLayout with a fake ClientApplication whose container was undefined. InboxEntry called useInboxUnreadCount() -> useApiClient() -> useService(), which reads context.container.resolve, so the one test in the file failed with TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'resolve'). The aggregate run ended 1 failed / 233 passed.

原因（实现者自述）
The application added InboxEntry to client/layouts/components/header-actions.tsx; it reads the unread count through the API client service. This template shell-translation test renders AppLayout directly with a minimal fake app and never provided that service or a QueryClient. tests/logic/client-shell.test.tsx, the other shell test, had been updated when the entry was added; this one was missed.
处理方式
Mocked @nocobase/app-plugin-notification-in-app/client/inbox in tests/components/layout-i18n.test.tsx so useInboxUnreadCount returns { data: 0 }. The translation assertions (breadcrumb, footer, accessible labels) are untouched; only the unrelated inbox data dependency is stubbed.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json

运行与验收记录

以下来自已有记录，不冒充 Agent 的根因分析。

阅读批注

验收失败记录

C01 必要子项未验证：'超过24小时未解决时提醒服务台' 无法实测。应用无任何超过24小时的工单（任务标注示例数据：是，但实际仅有 Super Admin 与用户自建数据），界面无回填/修改时间入口，创建接口拒绝 createdAt（400 Unrecognized key），无集合更新路由；overdue 由服务端真实时钟计算，24小时等待超出验收窗口。其余子项均已通过。

来源：verify-4/browser-acceptance/report.json

阅读批注

报告资料说明

本轮浏览器报告未通过。

实现者补充建议

来自可选过程笔记，不代表已独立确认；相同建议引用上方评审，其余保留原文。

建议 01 · test-maintenance

Template page tests must move with the page they cover

可自动化

Replacing a template-owned page (here client/pages/home/) leaves its template-owned test importing deleted modules. Because the failure is a module-resolution error at import time, the entire test file fails before any assertion runs, and it is easy to miss while the feature itself works.

建议改动

In the same change that replaces or removes a template page, rewrite or delete the matching tests/ file; verify by running that single test file before finishing.

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 02 · feature-completeness

A notification backend is not a feature until the shell links to it

需人工判断

Ticket assign/resolve/return/close each produced an inbox message and the API returned it with the right /tickets/:id target, yet the header had no bell and there was no /inbox route, so users never saw a clickable notification. Backend tests passed while the user-visible feature was absent.

建议改动

When a feature's value depends on a notification arriving, wire the recipient entry point (header badge plus the route the notification links to) in the same change, and assert the entry is reachable from the shell rather than only that the message row was written.

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 03 · validation

Validate uploaded bytes on the server, not only in the picker

可自动化

A client accept filter and a declared MIME type are hints, not checks: a corrupt file named .png uploaded and stored cleanly. Server-side content validation (magic bytes) is what makes the guarantee, and it must run before storage while still leaving the request body readable for the plugin’s own parser.

建议改动

For any upload whose type matters, add a server-side content check and a test that declares an allowed type over disallowed bytes; use a cloned request body so downstream size-limit and parse middleware are unaffected.

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 04 · test-maintenance

A new shell dependency requires updating every test that renders the shell

可自动化

Adding a data/service-dependent InboxEntry to header-actions.tsx broke one of the two tests that render AppLayout; only client-shell.test.tsx was updated at the time, and layout-i18n.test.tsx surfaced the gap only in the aggregate run.

建议改动

When adding a data- or service-reading component to shared shell code, grep the tests that render that shell component and provide the dependency (provider/mock) in all of them, then run those test files before finishing.

来源：Agent · retro.json

评审源产物：Run 38106778635 / attempt 1。发布重试不改变评审源产物编号。

以 IT 报修系统为测试场景，对 NocoBase3 基础框架的通知投递、周期任务、授权、文件上传与数据/路由基础五个能力单元进行评估。框架提供的渠道抽象、ScheduleExecutor、权限集解析、文件仓库路由与迁移/路由原语均被应用直接采用，指引（Skills）命中率高，尤其通知发送与调度器选型规则被准确执行。主要问题集中在应用侧：附件内容路由未按指引补认证、通知失败被静默吞掉；超时提醒正向场景因严格 schema 与缺少时钟注入而无法端到端验证。

业务验证背景 · 不计入框架评分
本 Run 验证轮次4
本 Run 工厂修复轮次3
已保留验证轮次2

仅本 Run 已归档记录；工厂修复轮次不等于 Agent 开发中全部试错次数。缺少 verify-1 时不能声称任务首轮通过。

首轮与修复过程 · 查看逐轮原始状态
轮次范围原始结论失败项
验证 2全量浏览器 QA报告未通过C01 我是IT服务台负责人，需要一个公司内部报修系统。员工提交问题、紧急程度和截图，服务台分派给工程师，工程师记录处理过程和解决办法，员工确认解决后关闭；仍未解决可以退回继续处理。 员工只看自己的工单，工程师只处理分派给自己的工单，服务台查看全部。新分派和处理结果及时通知相关人员，点击通知可以进入对应工单；超过24小时未解决时提醒服务台。首页展示待分派、超时工单和各工程师的工作量。
验证 4全量浏览器 QA报告未通过未记录 failed 项；不代表全部操作完成

NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 3

做得好的地方
F2 · 调度器指引的“无 UI 用 ScheduleExecutor”规则被准确执行

文档 / Skill / 示例 · @nocobase/jobs · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler

⌄

scheduler Skill 明确无需 UI 观测的周期任务不得使用 Scheduler，应使用 jobExecutorServiceToken 的 ScheduleExecutor；应用按此实现超时扫描任务。

提供的帮助
避免误用 Scheduler 插件导致无 UI 任务缺乏可视化与可观测性。
应保留的能力
保持该规则，并在 jobs 包 README 中同样明示以增强可发现性。
E13 E10 E11
做得好的地方
F3 · 通知在写入前集中校验并支持幂等

NocoBase3 插件 · @nocobase/app-plugin-notification · @nocobase/app-plugin-notification-in-app

⌄

NotificationService.send 校验 idempotencyKey/source/messages 并拒绝未支持属性，in-app provider 在落库前用用户管理服务校验收件人，降低脏数据风险。

提供的帮助
减少脏通知与重复通知，保证投递语义可控。
应保留的能力
可将校验约定补入 notification Skill 的错误对照表。
E4 E5
做得好的地方
F1 · 通知发送与收件箱指引可直接照用

文档 / Skill / 示例 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-notification-in-app · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-notification/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-notification

⌄

notification 的发送参考给出 resolve notificationServiceToken 与 messages.inbox（to/title/body/target route）完整片段，in-app 指引补全页面声明与导出组件；应用据此一次接线即通过验收。

提供的帮助
降低接入成本并提高通知功能一次实现成功率。
应保留的能力
保持该写法，并将其作为其他能力指引的模板。
E7 E8 E9

问题、误导与建议统一收录在问题与改进，不在此重复展示。

本次评审的覆盖限制
  • 仅存在评据 C01；所有能力单元的准则均记为 C01。
  • 浏览器验收截图仅来自 verify-4；verify-2 因缺少通知展示而失败。
  • 超时提醒的正向场景未在浏览器验收中覆盖（无超过 24 小时的样本且无法回填 createdAt）。
  • 快照未包含 3 个超出预算、非普通文件或不可读取的文件；未据此确认其实现。
评审基线与版本指纹

评审针对已封存候选补丁与当时的依赖；可能在原搭建内或之后单独补跑。与 PR 或 NocoBase3 当前最新提交不是同一概念。评审只读冻结材料，引用运行日志不代表评审者重新复现。此报告未执行最新上游源码复核。

E1 · app/server/providers/it-tickets/service.ts:1346–1379

应用工单服务通过 resolve(notificationServiceToken).send() 投递站内信，channel 名 inbox、target {type:route,path}。

  private async notify(
    recipients: readonly string[],
    message: {
      idempotencyKey: string;
      title: string;
      body: string;
      path: string;
    },
  ): Promise<void> {
    const to = [...new Set(recipients)].filter(Boolean);
    if (to.length === 0) {
      return;
    }
    let service: NotificationService | undefined;
    try {
      service = this.container.resolve(notificationServiceToken);
    } catch {
      return;
    }
    await service
      .send({
        idempotencyKey: message.idempotencyKey,
        source: { type: 'itTicket' },
        messages: {
          inbox: {
            to,
            title: message.title,
            body: message.body,
            target: { type: 'route', path: message.path },
          },
        },
      })
      .catch(() => undefined);
  }

import { useInboxUnreadCount } from '@nocobase/app-plugin-notification-in-app/client/inbox';
import type { ReactElement } from 'react';
import { inboxBadge, useDocumentTitleBadge } from '#components/inbox-badge';
import { InboxButton } from '#components/inbox-button';
import { TooltipProvider } from '#components/ui/tooltip';

import { ThemeSettings } from '../../theme/index.js';
import { UserMenu } from './user-menu.js';

/**
 * The inbox entry point. Ticket assignment and resolution notifications land in the inbox, and its badge is how the
 * recipient notices them; the button links to the `/inbox` route, which the notification-in-app plugin does not
 * provide itself. Nothing in this application produces inbox *decisions*, so only the unread count is shown.
 */
function InboxEntry(): ReactElement {
  const unread = useInboxUnreadCount();
  const badge = inboxBadge(0, unread.data ?? 0);
  useDocumentTitleBadge(badge?.text ?? null);
  return <InboxButton badge={badge} />;
}

/** The header's right-hand actions, shared by every authenticated page. */
export function HeaderActions(): ReactElement {
  return (
    <TooltipProvider>
      <div className='flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2'>
        <InboxEntry />
        <ThemeSettings />
        <UserMenu />
      </div>
    </TooltipProvider>
  );
}

E4 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-notification/dist/server/manager.js:211–248

NotificationService.send 校验 idempotencyKey、source、messages，并拒绝未支持的发送属性。

    async send(input) {
        await this.activate();
        validateNotificationIdempotencyKey(input.idempotencyKey);
        if (input.source !== undefined &&
            (!input.source ||
                typeof input.source.type !== 'string' ||
                !input.source.type.trim() ||
                (input.source.referenceId !== undefined &&
                    typeof input.source.referenceId !== 'string')))
            throw new Error('Notification source requires a type and an optional string referenceId.');
        if (!input.messages ||
            typeof input.messages !== 'object' ||
            Array.isArray(input.messages))
            throw new Error('Notification messages must be a Channel-to-message map.');
        for (const key of [
            'to',
            'channels',
            'content',
            'routing',
            'channelOverrides',
        ])
            if (key in input)
                throw new Error(`Unsupported notification send property "${key}".`);
        const channels = Object.keys(input.messages);
        if (channels.length === 0)
            throw new Error('At least one notification message is required.');
        const message = {};
        const expandedRecipients = [];
        for (const channel of channels) {
            await this.ensureRuntime(channel);
            const validated = this.channelManager.validateMessage(channel, input.messages[channel]);
            message[channel] = validated.message;
            const [provider] = this.channelManager.providerIdentities(channel);
            for (const recipient of validated.recipients)
                expandedRecipients.push({
                    channels: [{ channel, provider, recipient }],
                });
        }

    async boot() {
        const { container } = this.app;
        if (!container.has(notificationExtensionRegistryToken))
            return;
        const registry = container.resolve(notificationExtensionRegistryToken);
        const store = container.resolve(inAppNotificationStoreToken);
        const users = container.resolve(userAdministrationServiceToken);
        registry.registerChannel(createInAppChannelDefinition()).registerProvider(createDatabaseProviderDefinition({
            store,
            async recipientExists(userId) {
                return Boolean(await users.get(userId));
            },
        }));
    }
    shutdown() {
        this.realtimeTopic?.close();
        this.realtimeTopic = undefined;
        return Promise.resolve();
    }

            toSendInput({ values }) {
                const title = values.title?.trim();
                const body = values.body?.trim();
                if (!title || !body)
                    throw new Error('Title and Message are required.');
                const route = values.route?.trim();

        async createChannel() {
            return {
                type: 'in-app',
                validateMessage(value) {
                    if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value))
                        throw new Error('In-app message is required.');
                    for (const key of Object.keys(value)) {
                        if (!['to', 'title', 'body', 'target', 'actionUrl'].includes(key))
                            throw new Error(`Unsupported In-app message field "${key}".`);
                    }
                    const message = value;
                    const ids = typeof message.to === 'string' ? [message.to] : message.to;
                    if (!Array.isArray(ids) ||
                        !ids.length ||
                        ids.some((id) => typeof id !== 'string' || !id.trim() || id !== id.trim()))
                        throw new Error('In-app to must contain application user IDs.');
                    if (typeof message.title !== 'string' ||
                        !message.title.trim() ||
                        typeof message.body !== 'string' ||
                        !message.body.trim())
                        throw new Error('In-app title and body are required.');
                    return {
                        message: {
                            to: message.to,
                            title: message.title,
                            body: message.body,
                            target: validateNotificationTarget(message.target),
                        },

E7 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-notification/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-notification/references/sending-notifications.md:3–34

指引给出 resolve notificationServiceToken 与 messages.inbox 完整示例及错误语义。

Resolve `notificationServiceToken` from the shared application container. Select configured Channel names through `messages`; each value is the complete message for its Provider.

```ts
const notification = app.container.resolve(notificationServiceToken);
await notification.send({
  idempotencyKey: 'approval-123',
  source: { type: 'approval', referenceId: '123' },
  messages: {
    'system-email': {
      to: ['customer@example.com', 'reviewer@example.com'],
      subject: 'Approval result',
      html: '<p>Approved</p>',
    },
    'ops-feishu': {
      text: 'Approved',
      target: { type: 'url', url: 'https://example.com/main/approvals/123' },
    },
    inbox: {
      to: '123',
      title: 'Approval result',
      body: 'Approved',
      target: { type: 'route', path: '/approvals/123' },
    },
  },
});
```

Email requires a native email address or a non-empty readonly array of addresses, a subject, and text or HTML. Each address receives a separate email and retries independently. In-app messages require application user IDs in the same string/array shape, title, and body; final delivery checks that each user still exists. Webhook messages forbid `to`. The service does not resolve application users into email addresses or phone numbers and never defaults to the current user.

IM targets accept complete HTTP(S) URLs only. In-app targets accept a route without a deployment prefix or a complete HTTP(S) URL. React Router supplies the basename once for routes; URL targets use native links. Email links belong in text/HTML. Legacy `actionUrl` is ignored.

Unknown or disabled Channels and invalid messages or recipient formats reject the entire request before persistence or queue dispatch. Delivery-time failures are recorded independently. Reuse one stable `idempotencyKey` only for the same logical request; changing messages with that key is a conflict. Optional `source` adds business correlation. Use `getByIdempotencyKey`, `getNotification`, and `onStatusChanged` to observe results. A `retrying` Delivery is waiting for automatic execution; a terminal `failed` Delivery may be passed to `retryDelivery({ deliveryId, reason })`. An `unknown` Delivery must be checked externally before creating a new logical send.

E8 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-notification-in-app/SKILL.md:54–64

指引规定先注册 notification 再注册 in-app、由应用声明 inbox 页面并渲染导出组件。

# Workflow

1. Read [Inbox Integration Contract](references/inbox-integration.md) for package requirements, public surfaces, and runtime semantics.
2. Inspect the target application's Server plugin list, Client composition, authentication setup, `api.baseURL`, `api.realtimeURL`, and installed package versions.
3. Register `@nocobase/app-plugin-notification` before `@nocobase/app-plugin-notification-in-app/server` when the application needs the `in-app` Channel contribution.
4. Register `@nocobase/app-plugin-notification-in-app/client` in the Client composition root. The plugin contributes no pages: render `NotificationInAppInbox` inside `NotificationInAppProvider` in a page the application declares among its own routes.
5. In React components and custom Hooks, use `useApiClient()` from `@nocobase/app-client` for inbox reads and mutations, and `useService(realtimeClientToken)` for subscriptions. Outside React, resolve the corresponding tokens from the application's services or receive the clients explicitly. Do not reconstruct `/api` from the browser location or Portal base.
6. Keep HTTP state authoritative. On a validated `inbox.changed` event, realtime connection open, or window focus, trigger a bounded HTTP refetch.
7. Use the public `@nocobase/app-plugin-notification-in-app/realtime` entry for shared topic or event types; do not import Server internals.
8. Test allowed and denied users, mutations without a CSRF token, cross-site cookie writes rejected with `INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN`, pagination, custom API hosts, realtime invalidation, and reconnect recovery.
9. Run lint, typecheck, tests, and build for the plugin and affected application.

E9 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-notification-in-app/references/inbox-integration.md:25–43

契约说明 /client/inbox hooks、Provider 归属与收件人校验。

An application that renders its own inbox imports the React Query hooks from `/client/inbox`, with `@tanstack/react-query` installed and a `QueryClientProvider` above them:

```ts
import {
  inboxKeys,
  useInboxActions,
  useInboxItems,
  useInboxRefresh,
  useInboxUnreadCount,
} from '@nocobase/app-plugin-notification-in-app/client/inbox';
```

Prefer the hooks over calling the API helpers from components: `useInboxItems(pageSize)` pages by `nextPageToken`, `useInboxActions()` marks, deletes and reads all optimistically and refetches once settled, and `useInboxRefresh(extraTopics)` mounts the realtime, reconnection and focus invalidation once for the page. Extend `inboxKeys.all` invalidation for data the application attaches to messages by passing its own realtime topics to `useInboxRefresh` rather than subscribing separately.

Register `@nocobase/app-plugin-notification-in-app/client` in the application Client composition root. The plugin contributes no routes: an application that wants an inbox page renders `NotificationInAppInbox` inside `NotificationInAppProvider` in a page it declares among its own routes. Mounting the Provider in that page cleans up its realtime and focus listeners when navigation leaves the page.

## Final delivery validation

The database Provider checks the recipient through Authentication’s user administration service immediately before writing the inbox item. A missing user returns a non-retryable `recipient` failure and creates no inbox item or realtime event. A lookup failure remains a retryable storage failure. Custom hosts calling `createDatabaseProviderDefinition` must supply `recipientExists(userId)` backed by their authoritative user directory; do not use a permissive fallback.

  }

E11 · packages/@nocobase/jobs/dist/schedule/executor.js:34–60

ScheduleExecutor.addJob 在 setup 前登记规则、等待 schedule 后端就绪。

    async addJob(job, registerOnly = false) {
        this.assertOpen();
        const normalized = normalizeScheduleJob(job);
        this.handlers.set(normalized.name, job);
        if (registerOnly) {
            this.pending.delete(normalized.name);
            return {
                jobName: normalized.name,
                code: ScheduleReceiptCode.HandlerRegistered,
                message: 'Handler registered',
            };
        }
        if (this.state === 'created') {
            // Nothing is written before setup(): the rule is written there, before
            // any worker starts, so a due firing never meets a missing handler.
            this.pending.set(normalized.name, {
                job: normalized,
                payload: job.payload,
            });
            return upserted(normalized.name);
        }
        if (this.state === 'setting-up')
            await this.setupPromise;
        this.assertOpen();
        return this.upsert(normalized, job.payload);
    }
    async removeJob(name) {

原文件 SHA-256：3191f2bdda5d64efb0c7b486b298084e139d846f32d5beac8d892322ac20d117

E12 · packages/@nocobase/jobs/dist/schedule/executor.js:104–140

setup 幂等，失败时释放已获取资源并可重试。

    setup(options = {}) {
        this.assertOpen();
        this.setupPromise ??= this.runSetup(options.consume ?? true);
        return this.setupPromise;
    }
    shutdown() {
        this.shutdownPromise ??= this.runShutdown();
        return this.shutdownPromise;
    }
    async runSetup(consume) {
        this.state = 'setting-up';
        try {
            await this.backend.open();
            this.opened = true;
            for (const { job, payload } of this.pending.values()) {
                await this.upsert(job, payload);
            }
            this.pending.clear();
            if (consume)
                await this.backend.consume(this.runner);
            // shutdown() may have been called while this ran; it closes what was opened.
            if (this.state === 'setting-up')
                this.state = 'ready';
        }
        catch (error) {
            // A failed setup releases what it acquired and may be retried.
            if (this.state === 'setting-up') {
                this.setupPromise = undefined;
                this.state = 'created';
            }
            if (this.opened) {
                this.opened = false;
                await this.backend.close().catch((closeError) => {
                    this.logger?.error({ error: closeError }, 'Failed to release schedule resources after a failed setup');
                });
            }
            throw error;

原文件 SHA-256：3191f2bdda5d64efb0c7b486b298084e139d846f32d5beac8d892322ac20d117

E13 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/SKILL.md:12–16

指引明确无需 UI 观测的周期任务应使用 jobExecutorServiceToken 的 ScheduleExecutor。

## Decide Whether to Use Scheduler

The primary reason to define tasks through Scheduler is **UI observability for business administrators**. Developers define schedules and execution logic in code. Business administrators use the task list and detail pages the application builds on the Scheduler API to view schedules, track individual executions, and enable or disable tasks when authorized.

Tasks that do not need to be viewed and tracked through the UI must not be managed by the Scheduler plugin. Give recurring ones a `ScheduleExecutor` of their own from the application's jobs service (`jobExecutorServiceToken` in `@nocobase/app-server/jobs`), and one-off background work a `JobExecutor`; the application development Skill describes both. For administrator-visible tasks, deliver Client registration, access permissions, meaningful task names, and accurate final execution status together; successful server-side dispatch alone is incomplete.

  public async scanOverdueReminders(now: Date = new Date()): Promise<number> {
    const rows = await this.tickets().findMany({
      filter: this.overdueFilter(now),
      sort: (sort) => [sort.field('statusChangedAt').asc()],
      limit: 100,
    });
    if (rows.length === 0) {
      return 0;
    }

    const recipients = await this.serviceDeskUserIds();
    for (const row of rows) {
      await this.tickets().updateOne({
        filter: { id: row.id },
        values: { remindedAt: now },
      });
      await this.notify(recipients, {
        idempotencyKey: `it-ticket-${row.id}-overdue-${this.dayKey(now)}`,
        title: `工单 #${row.id} 超时未解决 / Ticket #${row.id} overdue`,
        body: `${row.title} 已超过 24 小时未解决 / Unresolved for over 24 hours`,
        path: `/tickets/${row.id}`,
      });
    }
    return rows.length;
  }

E16 · packages/@nocobase/authorization/dist/plugins/permission-sets/plugin.js:159–208

permissionSets 提供 resolveGrants/getEffective/toGrants 并合并有效权限集。

    async resolveAllGrants(input) {
        return this.toGrants(await this.getEffective(input));
    }
    grantsFor(identity) {
        // One store read per request: every derived answer comes from this promise.
        let sets;
        const effective = () => {
            sets ??= this.getEffective(identity);
            return sets;
        };
        let grants;
        const resolveAll = () => {
            grants ??= effective().then((resolved) => this.toGrants(resolved));
            return grants;
        };
        return {
            resolve: async (input) => (await resolveAll())
                .filter((grant) => this.resourceMatches(grant.resource, input.resource) &&
                grant.action === input.action)
                .map((grant) => ({ ...grant, resource: input.resource })),
            resolveAll,
            unrestricted: async () => this.hasUnrestrictedSets() &&
                this.setsAreUnrestricted(await effective()),
        };
    }
    hasUnrestrictedSets() {
        for (const protection of this.protections.values()) {
            if (protection.unrestricted)
                return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    setsAreUnrestricted(sets) {
        return sets.some((set) => this.protections.get(set.key)?.unrestricted);
    }
    toGrants(sets) {
        return sets.flatMap((set) => set.grants.flatMap((grant) => grant.actions.map((action) => ({
            source: {
                plugin: 'permission-sets',
                id: set.key,
                ...(set.title === undefined ? {} : { title: set.title }),
            },
            resource: grant.resource,
            action: action.action,
            ...(action.policy === undefined ? {} : { policy: action.policy }),
        }))));
    }
    getEffective(input) {
        return this.resolvePermissionSets(input);
    }

E18 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/SKILL.md:57–63

指引给出声明迁移、defineDatabasePermission/defineCompositeResource、route 保护与客户端 authz 的实现顺序。

1. Declare schema and relationships through self-contained migrations. Choose stable composite, page and data scope names. Keep authorization decisions out of migration code.
2. Declare reusable collection permissions with `defineDatabasePermission`; compose user-facing business operations as composite resources with `defineCompositeResource` from `@nocobase/authorization/core`. Explicitly name fields, relation capabilities and every collection a workflow touches.
3. In the owning provider's `boot`, opt collections in with `authz.database.collections.add`, register composites with `authz.compositeResources.define`, record access with `authz.recordAccess.define` and settings items with `authz.settings.add`. Then add a workspace subsection with `authz.ui.sections.add({ name, title, parent })` and list each composite or settings item in it with `authz.ui.place(reference, { section, group? })` (the permission workspace lists sections and subsections on the left and the selected subsection's resources, under optional `authz.ui.groups`, on the right; an unplaced composite or settings item goes under its default section's Other with a startup warning, and a misplaced one fails a development start). Pages are not registered on the server: the client route tree lists them. Release subject registrations at shutdown.
4. Protect every server route with authentication. For a simple single-scope Repository operation, keep `defineRepositoryApiRoutes` and add `authz.database.authorizeRepository` to map methods to composite actions; it reuses or initializes the request's authorization context. For workflows, persisted-state checks, multiple data scopes or custom responses, install `authz.middleware()`, call `c.get('authz').authorize()` once and bind every returned collection policy with `repository.withPolicy()`. Follow [Repository route integration](references/repository-routes.md).
5. Declare `authz` on every entry client route (nested pages inherit it; an omitted one defaults to root-only or `'skip'` with a development warning), add `useCan` for action visibility and obtain per-record eligibility from the server when needed. Handle pending and error states without displaying stale access.
6. Complete the accompanying permission configuration from the responsibility matrix: sets, page and action grants, selected data scopes, supported rules and intended assignments. Use seeds for fresh installations and authorized provisioning for existing installations; editing a seed does not update a running App. Resolve optional capabilities through their installed Skills and preserve unrelated administrator edits.
7. Verify denied as well as allowed requests through the production route factory and actual database policies. Then verify the UI with ordinary users and inherited memberships.

E19 · app/server/providers/it-tickets/service.ts:1292–1320

应用用 permissionSets.listAssignments/getEffective 判定 root 用户，业务可见性在服务层过滤。

  private async rootUserIds(): Promise<string[]> {
    if (!this.authz) {
      return [];
    }
    try {
      const assignments = await this.authz.permissionSets.listAssignments(
        this.rootSet,
      );
      return assignments
        .filter((assignment) => assignment.subject.type === 'user')
        .map((assignment) => assignment.subject.id);
    } catch {
      return [];
    }
  }

  private async userIsRoot(userId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!this.authz) {
      return false;
    }
    try {
      const sets = await this.authz.permissionSets.getEffective({
        principal: { type: 'user', id: userId },
      });
      return sets.some((set) => set.key === this.rootSet);
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

            content: {

                    schema: one ? uploadOneBodySchema : uploadManyBodySchema,

            ...apiErrorResponses,
            400: apiErrorResponse(400, `The body is not valid multipart (\`INVALID_MULTIPART\`) or ${one ? 'does not hold exactly one `file` (`INVALID_FILE`)' : 'holds no `file` or a `file` field that is not a file (`INVALID_FILES`)'}.`),
            403: apiErrorResponse(403, "The exposure's Policy does not allow creating records (`WRITE_FORBIDDEN`), or it depends on a principal and none was resolved (`PRINCIPAL_REQUIRED`)."),
            413: apiErrorResponse(413, `The body exceeds ${maxSize} bytes (\`BODY_TOO_LARGE\`).`),

E23 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md:245–258

指引明确没有内置内容嗅探/恶意扫描，业务策略需应用自行实现。

## Verify and handle failures

Verify upload → query → contentUrl → identical downloaded bytes, including batch upload, resource aliases and host prefixes. After materialization run the consuming App's typecheck/build and exercise upload, cancel/retry, remove, download and preview. Restricted files need anonymous, forbidden-user and permitted-user tests against both API and content routes. Inspectors check registration only.

For preview integration or changes, verify each of PDF/DOCX/XLSX/PPTX with real files in development and a served production build. Check relative same-origin URLs, an authorized and unauthorized session, cross-origin CORS success/failure, failed requests and invalid documents, and `download={false}`. Close or switch files while loading and after a failure: pending requests must abort, viewers must be destroyed, and the next file must render without stale state. Verify PDF in a headed browser: previewing and paging a PDF must keep the top-level URL and the session. In a headless browser without a PDF viewer, a download or an `about:blank` tab is a browser limitation: record the browser and its mode and retry headed before reporting a component defect. A login redirect points to the content route or the session. Unit mocks and a successful build do not establish document rendering fidelity.

- Upload defaults are 5 MiB single / 20 MiB batch for the whole multipart body, including overhead. Direct Server uploads have no HTTP limit; UI maxSize checks an individual file. The Client supplies the multipart boundary.
- Upload failures use the standard `/api` error body with domain `file`; branch on `error.reason` (`ApiClientError.reason` in the Client), never on `message`. A successful upload answers 201.
- BODY_TOO_LARGE (413) and UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE (415): reduce request size or adjust route limits; send `multipart/form-data`. INVALID_MULTIPART/INVALID_FILE/INVALID_FILES (400): send native File values and a nonempty batch. PRINCIPAL_REQUIRED (403): a Policy function needs a resolved principal.
- INVALID_FILE_COLLECTION: first compare the migration name, route collection and Server repository argument; then await collections.get(logicalName) and check the active connection and generated metadata. SQLite physical text does not imply a broken datetime definition. Fix the specific lookup, metadata or schema problem and retry the same upload; do not bypass File Repository. Use a new migration for corrections to already-merged history.
- STORAGE_URL_UNAVAILABLE (503): configure a capable disk or stream mode.
- FILE_COMMIT_UNCERTAIN / FILE_CLEANUP_FAILED: reconcile database records and stored objects before retrying; uploads have no idempotency key.

There is no built-in route authentication, row ACL, Range/206, ETag, conditional download, resumable upload, physical cleanup, content sniffing or malware scan. Metadata deletion retains objects; cancelled forms may leave unlinked files. Implement the policies required by the business, including referenced-file deletion and orphan cleanup.

E24 · app/server/routes/it-ticket-files.ts:86–145

应用在插件写入前用 magic bytes 校验上传为光栅图片并控制 multipart 大小。

/** Whether a screenshot is one this application serves: a real raster image, by content. */
async function isSupportedAttachment(file: File): Promise<boolean> {
  const declared = file.type.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (declared && !declared.startsWith('image/')) return false;
  const header = new Uint8Array(await file.slice(0, 16).arrayBuffer());
  return detectImageMime(header) !== null;
}

/**
 * Reject anything that is not a real raster image before the file plugin stores it.
 *
 * The plugin accepts whatever the caller sends and records the caller's `type`, so a file declared `image/png` but
 * holding arbitrary bytes would be stored and later served. Reading the multipart body from a clone lets the check run
 * before a byte reaches storage while leaving the request itself intact for the plugin's own size limit and parser.
 */
async function validateAttachmentUpload(
  context: Context,
  next: () => Promise<void>,
): Promise<void> {
  const contentType = context.req.header('content-type')?.toLowerCase() ?? '';
  if (!contentType.startsWith('multipart/form-data;')) {
    await next();
    return;
  }
  const declaredLength = Number(context.req.header('content-length') ?? '0');
  if (Number.isFinite(declaredLength) && declaredLength > MAX_MULTIPART_BYTES) {
    throw new ApiError({
      status: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
      reason: 'BODY_TOO_LARGE',
      domain: FILE_DOMAIN,
      message: 'Upload request body is too large.',
      httpStatus: 413,
    });
  }
  let form: FormData;
  try {
    // Read a clone: the original stream stays untouched for the plugin's own size limit and body parser, which run
    // after this middleware. Reading the original here would leave it locked for both of them.
    form = await context.req.raw.clone().formData();
  } catch {
    // The plugin's own multipart error is the better answer for a malformed body.
    await next();
    return;
  }
  const files = form
    .getAll('file')
    .filter((candidate): candidate is File => candidate instanceof File);
  for (const file of files) {
    if (!(await isSupportedAttachment(file))) {
      throw new ApiError({
        status: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
        reason: 'TICKET_UNSUPPORTED_ATTACHMENT',
        domain: FILE_DOMAIN,
        message:
          'Only PNG, JPEG, GIF, WebP or BMP screenshots can be uploaded.',
      });
    }
  }
  await next();
}

E26 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md:124–130

指引说明 content 路由公开，受限文件需应用对 <accessPath>/* 自行加认证授权。

`policy` is required and governs every action of the exposure, uploads included. It is a Repository Policy: `read`, `create`, `update` and `delete`, each `true`, `false`, or a rule node. A node that is `false` refuses that operation; a node with no `fields` accepts no caller-supplied field, which is what a metadata `createOne` needs before it will accept anything. Pass a function of the principal — `policy: (principal) => ({ ... })` — together with a `principal(context)` resolver to scope rows to the caller; without a principal the request is refused with 403.

An upload supplies no caller fields, so it binds a Policy derived from this one: the `create` scope and defaults are inherited and the field allowlist is replaced by the file columns. `create: false` therefore forbids uploading as well as creating metadata — decided, with the principal, before the body limit, the content type or the multipart body is read, so a refused upload answers `403 WRITE_FORBIDDEN` (domain `app`) and stores nothing — and a `create.defaults` of `{ ownerId }` is stamped onto uploaded rows — which is what keeps an uploaded file inside the same scope `findMany` reads. The content route under `accessPath` is the exception: it is public and this Policy does not reach it.

Content is GET `<accessPath>/<uuid>.<ext>` outside `/api`, omitting the dot when extensionless. Stream returns full bytes as an attachment; redirect returns a public storage URL or a five-minute signed URL. A disk without URL support requires stream mode; there is no automatic fallback.

These are public routes. For restricted files, register App-owned authentication and authorization on the paths each contribution owns before mounting it — `/<name>/<action>` per exposed API action, `<accessPath>/*` for content. Never `router.use('*', ...)` in a contribution router: contributions share the mounted router, so it also guards the SPA and every contribution mounted after yours. Check the operation and record/parent-record access; a login page, private disk, Client filter or Policy field allowlist is not authentication. Add scoped middleware and principal-based Policy to the generated routes first. If an existing contract needs an adapter, extend that module using the public Server manager and retain its authorization checks; missing built-in authentication alone is not a reason to replace the routes. Never trust browser-supplied ownership.

E28 · packages/@nocobase/db/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:86–130

## 2. Migrations and seeds

A migration creates structure. A seed inserts the records the application needs in order to run. Neither does the other's job. Sample or demonstration content is not install data: declare it as a sample seed, described below, never as a plain seed.

```ts
// database/main/migrations/202609020001_create_orders.ts
import { defineMigration, type MigrationDefinition } from '@nocobase/db';

const migration: MigrationDefinition = defineMigration({
  name: '202609020001_create_orders',

  async up({ builder }) {
    await builder.createCollection('orders', (collection) => {
      collection.increments('id');
      collection.string('orderNo', { length: 64, nullable: false });
      collection.string('status', { length: 32, nullable: false });
      collection.datetime('createdAt', { nullable: false });
      collection.unique('orderNo');
      collection.index('status');
    });
  },

  async down({ builder }) {
    await builder.dropCollection('orders');
  },
});

export default migration;
```

```ts
// database/main/seeds/202609030002_default_order_statuses.ts
import { defineSeed } from '@nocobase/db';

export default defineSeed({

      filter: { code: 'draft' },
      create: { code: 'draft', title: 'Draft' },
      update: { title: 'Draft' },
    });
  },
});

        .notNull()
        .defaultTo('normal');
      collection
        .string('status', { length: 16 })
        .notNull()
        .defaultTo('pending');
      collection.datetime('statusChangedAt').notNull();
      collection.string('reporterId', { length: 64 }).notNull();
      collection.string('assigneeId', { length: 64 }).nullable();
      collection.text('resolution').nullable();
      collection.datetime('resolvedAt').nullable();
      collection.datetime('closedAt').nullable();
      collection.string('closedById', { length: 64 }).nullable();
      collection.datetime('remindedAt').nullable();
      collection.datetime('createdAt').notNull();
      collection.datetime('updatedAt').notNull();

      collection.index('reporterId', { name: 'ix_it_tickets_reporter' });
      collection.index('assigneeId', { name: 'ix_it_tickets_assignee' });
      collection.index('status', { name: 'ix_it_tickets_status' });
    });

E32 · app/server/routes/it-tickets.ts:87–120

应用路由用 defineApiRoutes、auth.required() 与 ApiError 映射业务错误。

/** `auth.required()` guarantees a session; the narrowing is what the service needs. */
function principalOf(context: Context): TicketPrincipal {
  const session = (context as unknown as Context<AuthEnv>).get('auth');
  if (!session) {
    throw new ApiError({
      status: 'UNAUTHENTICATED',
      reason: 'IT_TICKET_UNAUTHENTICATED',
      domain: DOMAIN,
      message: 'A session is required.',
    });
  }
  return { userId: session.user.id };
}

export const itTicketApiRoutes: AppApiRouteContribution<Application> =
  defineApiRoutes((app) => {
    const router = new Hono();
    const auth = app.container.resolve(authenticationToken);
    const tickets =
      app.container.resolve<ItTicketService>(itTicketServiceToken);

    router.onError((error, context) =>
      apiErrorHandler(
        error instanceof ItTicketError ? toApiError(error) : error,
        context,
      ),
    );

    // Authentication is installed on each path this contribution owns, never on `*`: the shared `/api` router merges
    // every contribution, and a wildcard here would also guard routes registered after it.
    const secure = (path: string): void => {
      router.use(path, auth.required());
    };

E34 · packages/@nocobase/app-client/dist/plugins.js:399–434

app-client 规范化页面 authz 并给出缺省回退与告警。

function normalizeRouteAuthz(route, id, path, isPage, auth, context) {
    if ('access' in route) {
        throw new Error(`Client route "${id}" uses removed access; declare authz instead.`);
    }
    const value = 'authz' in route ? route.authz : undefined;
    if (value !== undefined) {
        if (!isPage)
            throw new Error(`Client route group "${id}" cannot declare authz.`);
        if (value === 'skip' || value === 'unrestricted')
            return value;
        if (!value ||
            typeof value !== 'object' ||
            !('resource' in value) ||
            !value.resource ||
            typeof value.resource !== 'object' ||
            typeof value.resource.type !== 'string' ||
            !value.resource.type.trim() ||
            typeof value.resource.id !== 'string' ||
            !value.resource.id.trim() ||
            typeof value.action !== 'string' ||
            !value.action.trim())
            throw new Error(`Client route "${id}" must use authz "skip", "unrestricted" or { resource: { type, id }, action }.`);
        return Object.freeze({
            resource: Object.freeze({ ...value.resource }),
            action: value.action,
        });
    }
    if (!isPage)
        return 'skip';
    if (context.ancestorAuthz !== undefined)
        return context.ancestorAuthz;
    // An omission never stops the application: a protected page stays closed to all but unrestricted identities.
    const fallback = auth !== 'required' ? 'skip' : 'unrestricted';
    if (isDevelopment())
        console.warn(`Client route "${id}" at "${path}" does not declare authz; using "${fallback}". Declare authz on this page: { resource: { type, id }, action } or "skip".`);
    return fallback;

Exports `itTicketServiceToken`.\n- `server/providers/it-tickets/provider.ts` — binds the service and schedules the overdue reminder job every 15 minutes.\n- `server/providers/it-tickets/sample-data.ts` — the demo users and tickets, registered on `sampleDataToken`, built only when the database was installed with `APP_SAMPLE_DATA=true`.\n- `server/routes/it-tickets.ts` and `server/routes/it-ticket-files.ts` — the HTTP surface; `server/routes/schemas.ts` holds the response schemas.\n\n## Things that are easy to get wrong here\n\n- **Job scope must be package-shaped.** `@nocobase/jobs` rejects a scope containing `:` or a second `/`. The reminder job uses `@nb3-factory/it-tickets`; `app/it-tickets` throws at startup.\n- **A cookie-authenticated write needs a trusted origin.** `auth.required()` runs Better Auth's CSRF check, so a write whose `Origin`/`Referer` is not the app's own origin is refused with `403 INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN`. Production sets `APP_PUBLIC_ORIGIN`; the integration test sets it too.\n- **The service is HTTP-free.** Its `ItTicketError` carries a numeric status for classification; the routes translate it to an AIP status. A service `409` (closed ticket / invalid state) becomes `FAILED_PRECONDITION`, i.e. HTTP `400`, so those routes declare `400`. Keep the route's `toApiError` and the declared responses in step.\n- **Roles are the app-owned `itTeamMembers` table**, not permission sets: `userId` plus `engineer` or `serviceDesk`. Root users count as service desk. Scoping is enforced in the service, and the `/api` routes only require a session.\n- **Demo users need a `username`.** Better Auth's `sign-in/username` looks users up by `username`, so an account created without one cannot sign in. The demo usernames are `zhang.san`, `li.si`, `wang.wu`, `zhao.liu`; the shared password is `DEMO_PASSWORD` in `sample-data.ts`.\n- **Overdue** means: not `closed` and not `resolved`, untouched for over 24 hours, and not reminded in the last 24 hours. The scan stamps `remindedAt` and notifies the service desk.\n\n## Example data\n\n`pnpm nocobase db sample` (development) loads what an install skipped. 1 项验收需要处理

A1 我是IT服务台负责人，需要一个公司内部报修系统。员工提交问题、紧急程度和截图，服务台分派给工程师，工程师记录处理过程和解决办法，员工确认解决后关闭；仍未解决可以退回继续处理。 员工只看自己的工单，工程师只处理分派给自己的工单，服务台查看全部。新分派和处理结果及时通知相关人员，点击通知可以进入对应工单；超过24小时未解决时提醒服务台。首页展示待分派、超时工单和各工程师的工作量。：未验证

本轮未完成

本轮不是完成交付。请查看本轮验收记录和问题记录。

验收失败记录

C01 必要子项未验证：'超过24小时未解决时提醒服务台' 无法实测。应用无任何超过24小时的工单（任务标注示例数据：是，但实际仅有 Super Admin 与用户自建数据），界面无回填/修改时间入口，创建接口拒绝 createdAt（400 Unrecognized key），无集合更新路由；overdue 由服务端真实时钟计算，24小时等待超出验收窗口。其余子项均已通过。

来源：verify-4/browser-acceptance/report.json

本轮浏览器报告未通过。

What was delivered

业务场景与交付说明

完整报修闭环

工单 #1 实际走完 pending→assign→processing→resolved→return→processing→resolve→closed，历史时间线完整可查。

严格的角色可见范围

员工仅见本人工单、工程师仅见被分派工单、服务台可见全部；越权直接访问工单或 /main/team 均被拒。

通知即时可达

新工单、分派、解决、退回、关闭均产生中英文通知，点击通知跳转到对应工单详情。

首页与附件质量

首页展示待分派、超时区块和各工程师工作量；合法截图上传并预览，损坏/不支持文件得到明确反馈。

01员工登录并提交标题、描述、紧急程度和截图
→
02服务台查看全部工单并分派给工程师
→
03工程师记录处理过程并填写解决办法
→
04员工确认解决并关闭，或退回继续处理
→
05相关人员收到通知并可点击进入工单
→
06首页汇总待分派、超时与工程师工作量

业务顺序示意，操作证据见逐项验收。

01

我是IT服务台负责人，需要一个公司内部报修系统。员工提交问题、紧急程度和截图，服务台分派给工程师，工程师记录处理过程和解决办法，员工确认解决后关闭；仍未解决可以退回继续处理。 员工只看自己的工单，工程师只处理分派给自己的工单，服务台查看全部。新分派和处理结果及时通知相关人员，点击通知可以进入对应工单；超过24小时未解决时提醒服务台。首页展示待分派、超时工单和各工程师的工作量。

环境受阻：唯一未能实测的必要子项是“超过24小时未解决时提醒服务台”。应用当前没有任何超过24小时的工单（示例数据缺失，初始仅有 Super Admin、零工单），界面无回填/修改时间入口，创建接口 POST /main/api/itTickets 明确拒绝 createdAt（400 Unrecognized key），且不存在集合更新路由（/main/api/itTickets:update 等均 404）；服务端按真实时钟计算 overdue（itTickets/itDashboard 返回 overdue 字段）。因此无法在验收窗口内触发正向超时场景，属测试准备/运行环境阻塞。其余子项均已通过真实浏览器操作验证。

A1 · 15 条操作 · 8 条观察 · 30 张证据
未验证
⌄

原始验收要求

我是IT服务台负责人，需要一个公司内部报修系统。员工提交问题、紧急程度和截图，服务台分派给工程师，工程师记录处理过程和解决办法，员工确认解决后关闭；仍未解决可以退回继续处理。 员工只看自己的工单，工程师只处理分派给自己的工单，服务台查看全部。新分派和处理结果及时通知相关人员，点击通知可以进入对应工单；超过24小时未解决时提醒服务台。首页展示待分派、超时工单和各工程师的工作量。

实际操作

  1. 以服务台管理员 nocobase 登录，确认离开登录页并进入 Home / Tickets / Team 业务界面
  2. 通过注册与 Team 角色管理创建并赋予角色：员工 Factory QA、员工 Factory QA Two、工程师 QA Engineer、工程师 QA Engineer Two
  3. 员工 Factory QA 用 New ticket 提交标题、描述、紧急程度 Urgent 并上传 sample.png，表单校验与预览正常
  4. 同一上传控件测试 corrupt.png（明确失败+格式提示）与 unsupported.exe（不支持提示）；并渲染/预览已上传的 sample.png
  5. 服务台打开工单 #1 执行 Assign，从工程师列表选择 QA Engineer，状态变更并写入时间线
  6. 工程师 QA Engineer 执行 Record progress 记录处理过程，再 Mark resolved 填写 Solution，状态变为 Awaiting confirmation
  7. 员工 Factory QA 执行 Return 填写退回原因，状态回到 In progress；工程师再次 process + resolve
  8. 员工 Factory QA 执行 Confirm and close 填写确认意见，状态变为 Closed，完整历史时间线可查
  9. 逐角色检查 Inbox 通知（新工单→服务台、分派→工程师、解决→员工、退回→工程师、关闭→工程师），点击通知进入 /main/tickets/1
  10. 员工2 打开 Tickets 仅见自己的 #2/#3，直接访问 /main/tickets/1 被拒回列表，访问 /main/team 报 Access denied
  11. 工程师1 打开 Tickets 仅见分派给自己的 #1，直接访问 /main/tickets/2 得 403/回列表，访问 /main/team 报 Access denied
  12. 工程师2 打开 Tickets 仅见分派给自己的 #2
  13. 服务台 Tickets 页测试 Status=In progress、Urgency=Urgent 过滤与关键字搜索，Scope=All tickets 可见全部工单
  14. 服务台首页确认展示待分派数量、Overdue (over 24 hours) 区块与各工程师工作量（open/total）
  15. 尝试构造超时工单以验证正向提醒：以服务台身份 POST /main/api/itTickets 附加 createdAt 被拒，且无可用时间回填入口，确认无法构造 >24h 工单

观察结果

  1. 工单 #1 完整生命周期：Pending assignment → Assign → In progress → Record progress → Mark resolved(Awaiting confirmation) → Return → In progress → process+resolve → Confirm and close → Closed；历史时间线包含每一步操作者与时间
  2. 角色可见范围（服务端与列表双重限制）：员工1 仅见 #1；员工2 仅见 #2/#3；工程师1 仅见 #1；工程师2 仅见 #2；服务台 Scope=All tickets 见 #1/#2/#3
  3. 越权直接访问：员工2 打开 #1 被回退到列表；工程师1 打开 #2 返回 403（itTickets/2）；员工与工程师访问 /main/team 均显示 Access denied
  4. 通知文案与跳转：'工单 #1 已分派 / Ticket #1 assigned'、'请确认是否解决 / Please confirm'、'需要继续处理 / Needs further work'、'工单 #1 已关闭 / Ticket #1 closed'，target 均为 route /tickets/1，点击后进入工单详情
  5. 附件：sample.png 上传成功并内联渲染（naturalWidth 32、HTTP 200、content-type image/png），corrupt.png 显示 'corrupt.png: Failed' 及格式提示，unsupported.exe 显示不支持格式提示
  6. 过滤与搜索：Status=In progress 仅返回 #2；Urgency=Urgent 仅返回 #1；搜索 'battery' 仅返回 #2，URL 分别带 ?status/?urgency/?q 参数
  7. 首页：服务台显示 Pending assignment、Overdue (over 24 hours) 区块、Engineer workload（含 open/total）；itDashboard 返回 pending/processing/resolved/closed/overdue/engineers
  8. 超时未实测：首页 Overdue 区块为 'No ticket is overdue.'，itTickets/itDashboard 的 overdue 均为 0；全量工单仅 #1(closed)/#2(processing)/#3(pending)，均创建于会话内，无法在窗口内达到 24 小时

来源：verify-4/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：not-verified

完整原始验收要求
